------------------------------------------------------------------------
r4349 | gbc | 2025-04-21 13:49:48 -0400 (Mon, 21 Apr 2025) | 10 lines

added prep_fstruct() for check_names() protection

There are many places where an fstruct is likely
loaded with complete garbage and then passed to
check_names() to deal with it.  All identifiable
instances of check_names() have now been preceded
by a call to prep_fstruct() where it looks like
garbage (rather than a previously loaded fstruct)
has been provided.  Hopefully this is more robust.

r4236 | gbc | 2024-03-21 18:02:00 -0400 (Thu, 21 Mar 2024) | 11 lines

re-labelling i,j,k for sanity

i was (mostly) general purpose,         -> ii,xx
j was (mostly) fc code channel index    -> jj
and k did things with phase cal tones.  -> kt
Introduced xx for polarization rather than ii
and jk (but not yet used).  In the chids_ids
era, the control block values loaded at jj may
not correspond to the correlation channel indices
any longer....

it makes some sense.  External use can be the same, but
it is best to nuke fset prior to calling get_filelist().

r4069 | gbc | 2023-09-30 12:33:33 -0400 (Sat, 30 Sep 2023) | 14 lines

Fixed automake distribution in chops land.

A few things; chmod -R u+rwx was unnecessarily making everything
executable.  (Obviously r is not needed as well not; w only if
one has screwed up permissions somehow--unnecessary, but no harm).

The libraries built from rync'd copies of files (because of setup)
do not need to have yet-another copy of the files added to the
distribution.  Careful use of nodist_ on the library sources and
EXTRA_DIST to put back the few actually unique files suffices.

The hops_spec document is out of date and not built without defining
a macro.  Put in a comment to make that clearer.

r4032 | gbc | 2023-09-19 11:42:08 -0400 (Tue, 19 Sep 2023) | 7 lines

FileInputStream() rep.with Files.newInputStream()

Apparently FileInputStream() was a total design failure of
early Java and they finally fixed it around Java 7 or 8.
(It overrides finalize() and marks it as something to be
lazy with on garbage collecting, causing massive issues.)

r3995 | gbc | 2023-06-09 16:39:39 -0400 (Fri, 09 Jun 2023) | 24 lines

Changes for 3 trivial new cf directives.

noautofringes and mbdrplopt are implemented, mod4numbering only got as
far as elimination of the external symbol max_seq_no in favor of explicitly
passing that value as an argument.  Implemented mod4 requires a new function
to replace what get_fileset() is currently doing...and specialized to the
task.  Note that get_fileset() is called at top-level, so there is no real
need to keep calling it to track the number of fringes.

Stepping through the process prompted a re-ordering of new_parameters.txt
to be more aligned with how one should actually work.  In the process a
number of extraneous comments or compiler warnings were removed.  The plot
generator, generate_graphs.c is in need of serious decomposition, but for
the current commit, very little actually was changed.

Some notes on progress are found in automake-modernize-notes.txt.  As of
python3.11 distcheck is broken due to egg-info into the source dir (which
is not writable per standards), but that is a mistake anyway to be fixed
next.

Turned on SVN Ids for current reference.
Everything was duplicated to chops as per S.O.P.

r3354 | gbc | 2021-09-14 15:42:14 -0400 (Tue, 14 Sep 2021) | 29 lines

Implemented field rotaton for circular pols.

Some discussion with Vincent is captured in the text file:
postproc/fourfit/parallactic-angle-correction.txt and the
changes are necessarily duplicated into chops.  Aside from
the function to "do the math", there are the usual changes
to the control file scheme with a new mount_type entry to
the control block, 4 types of mounts (including not-specified),
and associated parser and cblock manipulations.  The param
structure requires elevation in addition to the existing
par_angle variables (for ref/rem).

Note that the existing scheme just uses the first coefficient,
so the correction is a constant for the duration of the scan.
A revised version could/should be implemented at the cost of
more interface changes.

A new option for station_pol_mode, CIRC_PAREL was introduced
to be sure not to break the existing code, and in any case the
new code has not yet been tested as the development venue needs
to shift to a machine with data to do the relevant testing.
Also fixed the indents in norm_fx() as these had become broken.

All existing tests pass and the new feature is untested.

It also seems to me that the original 'I' mode was broken in
the transformation to DiFX.

r3347 | gbc | 2021-09-10 14:50:42 -0400 (Fri, 10 Sep 2021) | 29 lines

New code for freq_spacing of MBD calculus.

The underlying issue was that if data from certain channels
were excised by norm_fx() edits, those (virtual) channels were
still being used to set up the space for the MBD calculation.
(This makes no physical sense--why should channels that are
no longer providing data be providing input to the MBD answer?)
As a related matter, max555 solves for a maximum in MBD, but if
there is only one contributing channel...that is also bogus...and
thus the SBD value is substituted.  (The other option is 0.0, but
that might be mis-interpreted.)

Additional cases to chk_ff_3772pb.sh were added, slightly altering
the observed average values for amplitude; and the mb_win was narrowed
to match the sbd_win range.

Various comments were inserted in fill_208.c, freq_spacing.c,
generate_graphs.c, and in the other files, comments and edits
appropriate to ignoring frequency channels with no contributing
data were made.   In addition to apbyfreq[] to track the #APs
contributing to each channel, napbyfreq was also added to the
status structure as a surrogate for pass->nfreq.  A completely
refactored code might consider adjusting pass->nfreq, but the
way things are currently written that is not trivial to do.

Minor tweaks to the fine print at the bottom of the fourfit plot
to allow having a clue about certain internal variables.

r3336 | gbc | 2021-09-08 15:24:53 -0400 (Wed, 08 Sep 2021) | 17 lines

possibly have resolved the bw correction issue.

The underlying problem is that if bandpass edits are made,
channels that were constributing may no longer be doing that
and this affects ALL of integration time, amplitude and SNR.
The problem is that total_ap_frac ends up incremented for
the channels eventually discarded, and the way this variable
is accumulated makes it hard to also get the integration time
correct (which relies on the number of channels requested,
pass->channels, rather than the number of channels actually used).

This problem also bleeds into the MBD calculation, since the
discarded channels will have no data in them, but the MBD
solution process does not understand this.  (The MBD problem
is deeper, since one still gets an MBD with even one channel.)

r3324 | gbc | 2021-09-03 17:20:20 -0400 (Fri, 03 Sep 2021) | 10 lines

Introduced adjust_snr() called by make_plotdata().

As a consequence of the 'apply' functions, the SNR as
currently calculated (theoretically) may end up incorrect.
This function has been added (rather than jamming more code
into make_plotdata() to encapsulate those adjustments.

At the moment the function does nothing, but verifying what
does or doesn't break with adjustments is the next step.

being able to pass corrective data to fix of the correlation

the param and status structures being passed in, rather

r3291 | gbc | 2021-08-20 17:09:14 -0400 (Fri, 20 Aug 2021) | 30 lines

hacking up norm_fx with STATUS_AP_ACCOUNTING

In theory this version works as well as the last one.

An unmolested copy of norm_fx.c was captured in a previous
revision; this version is punctuated by #if..#endifs on the
STATUS_AP_ACCOUNTING macro.  The point is that status is
updated early in the process and in fact, prior to all edits
but ad hoc flagging (which is thus correct).  The notches and
passband functions may make significant spectral edits and the
eventual amplitude, SNR and time history of amplitude all need
correction.  Three choices (0, 1 and 2) move this accounting
later in the logic and (by inspection and) by the testsuite
in ff_testdata all appear to work.  Case 3 (after the spectral
edits) fails the chk_notches.sh test (since the tests are not
actually testing correctly anyway) and the result is still
not quite correct.  Committing this interim case as I must
leave this for at least a week...

With this commit we'll leave it at case 2 (status ap updates
immediately prior to the notches and passband corrections.

In the process, a header apply_funcs.h was added to remove
some of the compilation warnings, the Ad Hoc Flagging enabled
warning was turned off, since we seem to have that solidly on.

The 'pol' variable is at this point a rather poor choice for a
varible name since there are too many things with pol inside.

r3286 | gbc | 2021-08-18 17:07:10 -0400 (Wed, 18 Aug 2021) | 29 lines

Unwound the python library madness/dependencies back on.

automatic dependency was turned off and it does not appear
possible to turn it back on cleanly in the same build dir.

hops_nightly.sh has acquired a 'makeclean' option so that
when it runs tonight it may actually clean and build fourfit
properly everywherre.

made gratuitous changes to 3 fourfit includes so that the
passband changes propagate.  Probably not necessary, but
the gratuitous changes were in comments, so who cares.

import_hops.sh can now function as an executable in addition
to being a sourceable recipe.

chops/source/python_src/mk4_module and
chops/source/python_src/vex_module Makefile.am files were
modified so that the dfio and vex libraries that are built
in this module actually import sources rather than asking
libtool to go chasing them down elsewhere in the tree (where
they do not exist).

It is likely that there is a way to create a larger stub
for the extra python capability which then elminiates the
need to rebuild vex and dfio yet again (and rather produces
files that python can link to in a normal way).

r2968 | gbc | 2020-05-20 18:02:59 -0400 (Wed, 20 May 2020) | 12 lines

actually have an obscure purpose:

	Actually, while those aren't in active use by automake itself,
	they are the input files I made for the chops cmake build system
	to take and configure into the automake Makefile.am files (yes
	it is silly to build the automake files using cmake, but it
	was less work than doing it by hand for each module).  While I
	haven't used them lately, they are still useful if I ever need to
	re-generate the automake Makefile.am files from scratch, though
	admittedly this probably won't happen anytime in the near future.

Added the -e option to estimate processing time.  Supported by
accounting changes in sub/util/report_times.c and include/mk4_util.h,
and locally in parse_cmdline.c and fourfit.c.  The latter will bail
from full processing in the presence of the estimation option.

r251 | gbc | 2011-05-16 17:36:08 -0400 (Mon, 16 May 2011) | 12 lines

Added gen_new_chan_id.c as hook to channel name manipulations.
fourmer.c gets calls to relabel the channels via relabel_chan_def().
do_record_merge.c does it via relabel_chan_ids().  Both go to a common
call gen_new_chan_id() which is a hook for all things future.

In addition, for do_record_merge.c I fixed up a few minor issues
(missing includes, unused variables) and added symbolic names for
things that might change in the future.  Some of the while() loops
were completing without success, but failure wasn't always caught.

This still needs work and rigorous testing....
